====================================================================================================
MOVSD STRUCT COPY ANALYSIS
====================================================================================================

Ghidra's decompiler cannot analyze MOVSD string copy instructions, producing
garbled bVar*-8 pointer arithmetic or for-loop decompositions. These can be
fixed by replacing them with explicit MOV instructions in code caves.
